// request pbjs bids when it loads
  pbjs.que.push(function() {
    pbjs.rp.requestBids({ 
callback: callAdserver, gptSlotObjects: gpt_slot_objects.filter(function(element, index) { 
return (element) ? true : false; }) });
});

// failsafe in case PBJS doesn't load
setTimeout(function() {
callAdserver(gpt_slot_objects);
}, 2000);
});

function callAdserver(gptSlots) {
    if (pbjs.adserverCalled) return;
    pbjs.adserverCalled = true;
    googletag.pubads().refresh(gptSlots);
  }
  
   function removeElement(classname) {
   var elem = document.getElementsByClassName(classname)[0];
   return elem.parentNode.removeChild(elem);
 }

        if (!$(this).parent(".cb-kabar-haji-2026").hasClass("cb-kabar-haji-2026--small")) {
            $(this).parent(".cb-kabar-haji-2026").addClass("cb-kabar-haji-2026--small");

            $(this).attr("dtr-sec", "hide");
            $(this).attr("dtr-act", "hide");

            $(this).attr("dtr-sec", "show");

        }
    });

    <div class="pos-relative mgt-4 mgb-16">
        <div class="slider-snap itp__articleContainer" id="cb_flash" data-inf data-container="pilihan">
        </div>
        <button class="slider-snap__btn slider-snap__btn--prev" type="button" data-inf-left="" onclick="onPrev('pilihan')">
            <i class="icon icon--sm icon-chevron-left"></i>
        </button>
        <button class="slider-snap__btn slider-snap__btn--next" type="button" data-inf-right="" onclick="onNext('pilihan')">
            <i class="icon icon--sm icon-chevron-right"></i>
        </button>

<script>
    function onPrev(name) {
        const container = document.querySelector(`[data-container="${name}"]`);
        const childWidth = container.children[0].offsetWidth;
        container.scrollLeft -= childWidth;
    }

    function onNext(name) {
        const container = document.querySelector(`[data-container="${name}"]`);
        const items = Array.from(container.querySelectorAll('.itp__boxFlashItem'));

        if (!items.length) return;

        const scrollLeft = container.scrollLeft;
        const maxScrollLeft = container.scrollWidth - container.clientWidth;

        if (scrollLeft >= maxScrollLeft - 2) {
            return; 
        }

        let nextItem = null;

        for (let item of items) {
            if (item.offsetLeft > scrollLeft + 1) {
                nextItem = item;
                break;
            }
        }

        if (!nextItem) return;

        container.scrollTo({
            left: nextItem.offsetLeft,
            behavior: 'smooth'
        });

        setTimeout(() => {
            playVisibleThumbnailsOnce(container);
        }, 200);
    }
    
    function getVisibleItemsFromScroll(container) {
        const items = Array.from(container.querySelectorAll('.itp__boxFlashItem'));

        const scrollLeft = container.scrollLeft;
        const containerWidth = container.offsetWidth;

        return items.filter(item => {
            if (item.classList.contains('itp__boxFlashButton')) {
                return false;
            }

            const itemLeft = item.offsetLeft;
            const itemRight = itemLeft + item.offsetWidth;

            return (
                itemLeft >= scrollLeft &&
                itemRight <= scrollLeft + containerWidth
            );
        });
    }

    const playedItems = new WeakSet();

    function playVisibleThumbnailsOnce(container) {
        const visibleItems = getVisibleItemsFromScroll(container);

        if (!visibleItems.length) return;

        const videoItems = visibleItems.filter(item => {
            const img = item.querySelector('.itp_img-flash');
            return img && img.getAttribute('data-thumbnail');
        });

        if (!videoItems.length) return;

        const targetItem = videoItems[videoItems.length - 1];

        if (playedItems.has(targetItem)) {
            return;
        }

        playedItems.add(targetItem);

        const imgEl = targetItem.querySelector('.itp_img-flash');

        if (imgEl && imgEl.getAttribute('d-loaded') === '1') {
            playAnimatedThumbnail(imgEl);
        }
    }

    function getVisibleItems() {
        const container = document.querySelector('.itp__articleContainer');
        const items = Array.from(container.querySelectorAll('.itp__boxFlashItem'));

        return items.filter(item => {
            const rect = item.getBoundingClientRect();
            const containerRect = container.getBoundingClientRect();

            return (
                rect.left >= containerRect.left &&
                rect.right <= containerRect.right
            );
        });
    }

    const animatedImages = new Map();

    function playAnimatedThumbnail(imgEl) {
        const wrapper = imgEl.closest('.itp_ratiobox-flash');
        const animated = imgEl.getAttribute('data-thumbnail');
        const original = imgEl.getAttribute('data-src');

        if (!wrapper || !original) return 0;

        if (!animated || animated.trim() === "" || animated === "undefined") {
            wrapper.style.backgroundImage = `url("${original}")`;
            return 0;
        }

        if (animatedImages.has(wrapper)) {
            clearTimeout(animatedImages.get(wrapper));
        }

        wrapper.style.backgroundImage = `url("${animated}")`;

        const duration = 6000;

        const timeoutId = setTimeout(() => {
            wrapper.style.backgroundImage = `url("${original}")`;
        }, duration);

        animatedImages.set(wrapper, timeoutId);

        return duration; 
    }

    function autoNextThumbnailOnce() {
        const visibleItems = getVisibleItems();
        if (!visibleItems.length) return;

        const readyItems = visibleItems.filter((item) => {
            const img = item.querySelector(".itp_img-flash");
            return img && img.getAttribute("d-loaded") === "1";
        });

        if (readyItems.length !== visibleItems.length) {
            setTimeout(autoNextThumbnailOnce, 500);
            return;
        }

        let index = 0;

        function playNext() {
            if (index >= readyItems.length) return;

            const item = readyItems[index];
            const imgEl = item.querySelector(".itp_img-flash");

            let delay = 0;

            if (imgEl) {
                delay = playAnimatedThumbnail(imgEl);
            }

            index++;

            const MIN_DELAY = 500;

            setTimeout(playNext, delay || MIN_DELAY);
        }

        playNext();
    }

    function initAnimatedThumbnailInview(el) {
        let hasPlayed = false;
        const items = document.querySelectorAll(el);

        if (!items.length) return;

        inviewHorizontal(items, () => {
            if (!hasPlayed) {
                hasPlayed = true;
                autoNextThumbnailOnce();
            }
        });
    }

    function inviewHorizontal (el, isIntersectCallback) {
        const container = document.querySelector('.itp__articleContainer');
        const items = el;

        const options = {
            root: container,
            rootMargin: '0px',
            threshold: 0.1
        };

        const callback = (entries, observer) => {
            entries.forEach((entry) => {
                if (entry.isIntersecting) {
                    observer.unobserve(entry.target);
                    if (isIntersectCallback) {
                        isIntersectCallback(entry.target)
                    }
                }
            });
        };

        const observer = new IntersectionObserver(callback, options);
        items.forEach(item => observer.observe(item));
    }

    function lazyImageHorizontal(el){
        inviewHorizontal(document.querySelectorAll(el), async function (target) {
            const item = $(target)
            const imgFlash = item.find('.itp_img-flash')
            let ratio = imgFlash.attr('data-ratio');
            let defaultImg = 'https://cdn.detik.net.id/detik2/images/default-' + (ratio ? ratio : 11) + '.gif';

            imgFlash.on('error', function () {
                imgFlash.attr('src', defaultImg);
            });

            imgFlash.hide();
            imgFlash.closest('.lqd').css("background-image", "url(" + imgFlash.attr('data-src') + ")");
            imgFlash.attr('src', imgFlash.attr('data-src'));
            imgFlash.attr('d-loaded', 1)

            var $parent = item.find('.media__link');

            if($parent.hasClass('media__link')) {
                const videoId = $parent.attr('data-id')
                const video = FlashPilihan.videos.find(video => video.id === videoId)
                const detikBigDataSdk = await FlashPilihan.detikBigDataSdk()

                $parent.attr({
                    'dtr-evt': "box flash",
                    'dtr-sec': "video flash",
                    'dtr-act': "video flash",
                    'onclick': "_pt(this)",
                    'dtr-idx': video.number,
                    'dtr-id': video.id,
                    'dtr-ttl': video.title,
                    'dtr-src': (typeof video.pilihan !== 'undefined') ? '' : FlashPilihan.srcModel
                })

                if (detikBigDataSdk) {
                    detikBigDataSdk.collectEvent('show', {
                        enter_from_doc_id: '',
                        doc_id: videoId,
                        spm: `desktop$##$detikcom$##$boxflash$##$${video.number}`
                    });
                }
            }
        })
    }

        get size () {
            const total = 30
            const currentCount = this.videos.length

            return total - currentCount
        },
        excludeProgramIds: ["170724519", "190313542"],
        randomIndex: -1,
        srcModels: ['modelA', 'modelB'],
        get srcModel () {
            return this.srcModels[this.randomIndex]
        },
        get excludeDocs () {
            const excludeStrVideosId = helper.getCookie('__vids')

            const videosIdTahan = this.videos.map(function (video) {
                return video.id
            })

            if (! excludeStrVideosId) {
                return videosIdTahan
            }

            const excludeVideosId = JSON.parse(excludeStrVideosId)
            return excludeVideosId.concat(videosIdTahan)
        },
        get url () {
            const baseUrlRecs = [
                'https://recg20.detik.com/article-recommendation',
                'https://rech20.detik.com/article-recommendation'
            ]

            const baseUrlRec = baseUrlRecs[this.randomIndex]

            return `${baseUrlRec}/wp/${this.cookieDtma}`
        },
        get cookieDtma () {
            const dtma = helper.getCookie('__dtma')

            if (! dtma) {
                return '-'
            }

            const splitted = dtma.split('.')

            return splitted.slice(0, 3).join('.')
        },
        articleTemplate (row) {
            return `<article class="slider-snap__item itp__boxFlashItem">
                        <div class="media media--image-radius media--text-overlay block-link">
                            <div class="media__image mg-0">
                                <div class="media__icon media__icon--top-right">
                                    <i class="icon icon--xs icon-play-bg"></i> ${helper.duration_pretty(row.duration)}
                                </div>
                                <a href="${row.videourl}?source=pilihan&from=${encodeURIComponent(window.location.href)}"
                                    data-id="${row.id}"
                                    class="media__link">
                                    <span class="ratiobox ratiobox--9-16 lqd itp_ratiobox-flash">
                                        <img class="itp_img-flash"
                                            src="https://cdn.detik.net.id/detik2/images/default-916.gif"
                                            data-src="${row.imageurl}?w=250"
                                            data-ratio="916"
                                            alt="${row.title}"
                                            title="${row.title}"
                                            data-thumbnail="${row.animated}">
                                    </span>
                                </a>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </article>`
        },
        async request () {
            return await fetch(this.url, {
                method: 'POST',
                headers: {
                    'Content-Type': 'application/json'
                },
                body: JSON.stringify({
                    size: this.size,
                    excludeProgramIds: this.excludeProgramIds,
                    excludeDocs: this.excludeDocs,
                    isVertical: true
                })
            })
        },
        async getData () {
            const req = await this.request()
            const data = await req.json()
            let indexArticle = 0
            let dataPilihan = [];

            this.videos.forEach((video) => {
                dataPilihan.push({
                    duration: video.duration,
                    imageurl: video.image,
                    videourl: `https://20.detik.com${video.slug_20detik}`,
                    number: indexArticle,
                    id: video.id,
                    title: video.title,
                    animated: video.animated,
                    pilihan: true
                })
            })

            let allData = [];
            const dataRecs = data.body;

            allData = dataPilihan.concat(dataRecs)
            this.videos = allData

            let articlesHtml = allData.map((row, i) => {
                indexArticle += 1
                row.number = indexArticle
                return this.articleTemplate(row)
            }).join('')

            articlesHtml += `
                <article class="slider-snap__item slider-snap__item--index itp__boxFlashButton">
                    <div class="media media--image-radius block-link">
                        <div class="media__image mg-0">
                            <span class="ratiobox ratiobox--9-16"></span>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    <div class="media__text">
                        <a dtr-evt="box flash" dtr-sec="lihat semua" dtr-act="lihat semua" onclick="_pt(this)"                            href="https://20.detik.com/flash" class="media__link font-semibold">
                            <i class="icon icon--sm icon-chevron-right mx-auto mgb-16"></i>
                            Lihat Lebih Banyak
                        </a>
                    </div>
                </article>`

            $('.itp__articleContainer').prepend(articlesHtml)
            helper.lqd_img('.lqd')
        },
        detikBigDataSdk () {
            if (typeof detikBigDataSdk !== 'undefined') {
                return Promise.resolve(detikBigDataSdk)
            }

            if (typeof detikBigDataSdk === 'undefined') {
                return new Promise((resolve) => {
                    const checkInterval = 100
                    let waitedTime = 0

                    const intervalId = setInterval(() => {
                        waitedTime += checkInterval

                        if (typeof detikBigDataSdk !== 'undefined' || waitedTime >= 3000) {
                            clearInterval(intervalId)
                            resolve(detikBigDataSdk)
                        }
                    }, checkInterval)
                })
            }

            return Promise.resolve()
        },
        async init () {
            this.randomIndex = Math.floor(Math.random() * this.srcModels.length)
            const observer = new IntersectionObserver(function (entries) {
                entries.forEach(async function (entry) {
                    if (entry.isIntersecting) {
                        observer.disconnect()
                        await FlashPilihan.getData()
                        lazyImageHorizontal('.itp__boxFlashItem');
                        initAnimatedThumbnailInview('.itp__boxFlashItem');
                    }
                })
            }, {
                rootMargin: "0px 0px 300px 0px"
            });

            observer.observe(document.querySelector('.itp__articleContainer'))
        }
    }

    $(function () {
        FlashPilihan.init()
    })

<script>
var loadScriptAsync = function(uri){
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    var tag = document.createElement('script');
    tag.src = uri;
    tag.async = true;
    tag.onload = () => {
      resolve();
    };
  var firstScriptTag = document.getElementsByTagName('script')[0];
  firstScriptTag.parentNode.insertBefore(tag, firstScriptTag);
});
}
var scriptLoaded = loadScriptAsync('https://newrevive.detik.com/delivery/asyncjs.php');

scriptLoaded.then(function(){
// channelbox year in review
    //document.getElementById("rv_cb_g20").setAttribute("data-revive-zoneid", rv_cb_g20);

 function rvCallbackBanner(data){

  }

 }

function checkBannerAvailable(dataDetail, checkZone, callFunc, available = false){
	try {
		var show = available ? 1 : 0;
		if(show == 0){
          if (dataDetail.zoneid == checkZone && dataDetail.bannerid == show) {
            callFunc();
            console.log('zone id adjustment : ' + checkZone);
          }
        }
        if(show == 1){
          if (dataDetail.zoneid == checkZone && dataDetail.bannerid >= show) {
            callFunc();
            console.log('zone id adjustment : ' + checkZone);
          }
        }
	} catch(e) {
		console.log(e);
	}
}

</script>
